# Archive Correction Probe - 2026-08-29

Read-only I-0 probe over cached IEM AFOS ZIP responses. No network fetches were made by this script, and it writes only this evidence document.

## Inputs

- Cache directory: `/home/jon/.local/share/breezy/archive/settlement-alignment-cache`
- Expected scope: `polymarket_us` CLI station-years 2021-2025
- Expected AFOS ZIPs found: 25/25
- Extra ZIPs present but excluded from the scoped probe: 5
- Product splitter and issuance recovery reused from `scripts/analysis/settlement_alignment_study.py`.
- Parser/classifier reused from `breezy.normalize.cli_parse.parse_cli_product` and `breezy.normalize.classify.classify_issuance`.

## Headline Results

- FINAL correction-candidate groups: 469
- FINAL correction-candidate later-product pairs: 496
- Candidate evidence split: BBB CCx=14, free-text-only=1, neither=481
- Candidate groups with any later-product correction evidence: BBB CCx=13, any evidence=14
- Candidate value comparison: values differ=12, byte-different but value-identical=472
- Candidate unresolved issuance deltas: 0
- Products with issuance instant from neither filename nor ISSUED line: 0
- Per-station-year yield min/median/max: 0.9836 / 1.0000 / 1.0000
- 0.95 yield floor verdict: supported

## Correction Base-Rate Honesty

Archive duplicate-FINAL candidate-group rate: 469/9096 parseable final station-days = 5.16% (Wilson 95% CI 4.72%-5.63%). This is a broad supersession-candidate rate, not the same denominator as the live corrected-final count.

Archive positional `CCx` correction-candidate group rate: 13/9096 = 0.14% (Wilson 95% CI 0.08%-0.24%).

Archive any-evidence correction-candidate group rate: 14/9096 = 0.15% (Wilson 95% CI 0.09%-0.26%).

Live-side reference from `docs/evidence/settlement_alignment_2026-08-25.md:32`: 1 corrected final out of 8 MDW finals = 12.50% (Wilson 95% CI 2.24%-47.09%).

The archive-vs-live contrast is a hypothesis with very wide uncertainty, not a settled finding. The archive `CCx` point estimate is much lower than the live MDW point estimate, but this probe does not by itself prove the archive is lossy for corrections.

## Candidate Delta Distribution

Delta seconds min/median/max/mean: 0 / 3300 / 123120 / 6367.1.

## What This Means For The Plan

- Item 7: The single-year yield distribution is 0.9836 min, 1.0000 median, 1.0000 max. A 0.95 floor is supported by this 2021-2025 cache; the observed minimum also leaves room to evaluate a stricter floor separately.
- Item 8: Monthly counts are reported above. The low-count flag uses the raw station-month counts requested here; it is a gap detector, not a month-length normalization. The month completeness table separately lists every month with fewer parseable FINAL days than calendar days.
- Item 9: Correction candidates are reported with Wilson uncertainty and compared to the live MDW 1/8 rate only as an uncertain hypothesis.
